Existing methods perform iterative retrieval and generation, letting accumulated context determine what to investigate next, which can overexploit locally promising evidence and fail to cover distinct semantic regions under a fixed budget. To address this, we cast deep research over data lakes as a budgeted search problem and present Baikal - a framework that clusters heterogeneous evidence into semantic regions, then searches over them adaptively to balance exploration and exploitation. Within each selected region, Baikal generates and investigates region-grounded subquestions, using finding quality as rewards to update region-level value estimates and guide search under policies ranging from random and LLM-guided selection to Bayesian $ε$-greedy and UCB. We evaluate Baikal on 15 queries each over HybridQA and TAT-QA data lakes containing 10,993 and 2,757 tables, respectively, together with 227K Wikipedia passages and 13K financial report passages. We assess research quality with a new rubric covering groundedness, relevance, diversity, and utility, and use GPT-5-mini to score Baikal and strong baselines, including DeepSearcher and an OpenCode research agent with retrieval and clustering variants. Across both data lakes, Baikal performs strongly under several region-selection policies; its best configuration improves report scores over the strongest baselines by 28% on HybridQA and 36% on TAT-QA. Our analyses attribute these gains to organizing and exploring semantic evidence regions, which improves groundedness and diversity and yields more useful findings under the same subquestion budget. These results demonstrate the value of structured semantic exploration for systematic research and discovery over heterogeneous data lakes.

</details>


### [106] [VeriSkill: A Self-Evolution Framework for Program Verification Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27733)
*Changguo Jia,Tianqi Zhao,Zhiyou Xiao,Weiming Zhang,Minghui Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VeriSkill 是一种面向程序验证的 LLM 技能自我进化框架，通过将验证失败归因于技能缺陷、提炼诊断签名并迭代优化技能，实现了在多种验证工具和 LLM 后端上的持续性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在使用 LLM 代理自动化程序验证时，需要生成规格、注解、辅助引理和工具调用等，这依赖可复用的技能。然而现有的技能进化方法难以在程序验证任务中可靠定位技能导致的失败，也难以从验证器的模糊反馈中提取可操作的信号，导致进化效果不佳。

Method: VeriSkill 通过以下步骤实现技能自我进化：① 将验证失败归因于具体的技能缺陷；② 从失败轨迹中提炼出诊断签名（可复用的经验教训）；③ 基于这些签名生成候选技能修订；④ 只接受在保持程序语义不变的前提下提升验证成功率的修订，形成闭环迭代。

Result: 在多个程序验证工具、不同的代理框架以及多种 LLM 后端上进行实验，VeriSkill 相较于所有基线方法均表现出更高的验证成功率和更快的收敛速度，验证了其跨工具和跨模型的通用性与有效性。

Conclusion: VeriSkill 通过精准的失败归因与可复用的诊断学习，实现了 LLM 代理在程序验证任务中的自我进化，显著提升了自动化验证的可靠性和效率，为后续基于 LLM 的软件验证研究提供了可扩展的框架。

Abstract: Automating program verification with LLM agents requires generating specifications, annotations, auxiliary lemmas, and tool invocations, all of which depend on reusable skills. A natural remedy is skill self-evolution: distilling skills from trajectories and refining them through feedback. However, existing evolution methods struggle with program verification tasks because they cannot reliably identify skill-specific failures or extract actionable signals from opaque verifier feedback. In this paper, we propose VeriSkill, a self-evolution framework built for program verification. It attributes verification failures to skill deficiencies, distills diagnostic signatures into reusable lessons, and iteratively refines candidate skills, admitting only revisions that improve verification performance while preserving program semantics. Experiments show that VeriSkill consistently outperforms all baselines across multiple verification tools, agent frameworks, and LLM backends.

</details>


### [107] [SpecCal: Ambiguity-Aware Candidate Calibration for Infrared Spectrum-Based Molecular Structure Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27788)
*Yixuan Chen,Bo Liu,Yusen Tan,Guokun Yang,Wenjie Du,Jun Xia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了无需训练的SpecCal框架，对现有IR‑to‑分子模型的候选集合进行再排序并加入光谱一致的结构，以提升IR光谱到分子结构的重建准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IR光谱信息有限，导致不同分子在光谱上高度相似，现有模型仅生成基于生成偏好的候选集合，难以覆盖所有满足光谱约束的真实结构，需一种方法在不改动模型的前提下提升候选质量。

Method: SpecCal是一种训练无关、模型无关的候选校准框架。它在已有模型输出的候选分子上进行再排序，并通过光谱一致性评估生成额外的结构合理的候选，以扩充并优化候选集合。框架采用plug‑and‑play方式，无需参数更新即可与多种基线模型集成。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，SpecCal对不同基线模型均实现了top‑k重建率的显著提升，且在SMILES和scaffold层面均表现出更高的准确性。进一步分析表明，针对光谱歧义进行候选校准是提升IR‑to‑分子重建的有效手段。

Conclusion: SpecCal通过光谱一致性驱动的候选再排序与扩充，提供了一种实用且易于集成的方式，显著改善了在光谱歧义条件下的分子结构推断，为IR光谱到分子预测提供了新的校准思路。

Abstract: Inferring molecular structures from infrared (IR) spectra is a fundamental yet challenging problem. A key difficulty is that an IR spectrum provides limited structural information: different molecules may share similar functional groups and local vibrational patterns, leading to highly similar spectral responses. Thus, even when an observed spectrum has a unique underlying structure, reconstructing it from the spectrum remains ambiguous. Existing IR-to-molecule models usually generate a ranked set of candidate molecules, but this set is largely determined by the model's learned generation preference and may not fully capture the structures that best satisfy the observed spectral constraints. To address this limitation, we propose SpecCal, a training-free candidate calibration framework for IR-to-molecule prediction. SpecCal operates on the candidate outputs of existing base models and improves the prediction set by re-ranking current candidates while introducing additional structurally plausible alternatives guided by spectral consistency. The framework is plug-and-play and model-agnostic, requiring no parameter updates for integration with diverse base models. Experiments on multiple benchmarks show that SpecCal consistently improves top-k reconstruction at both SMILES and scaffold levels across different base models. Further analyses demonstrate that calibrating candidate sets under spectral ambiguity provides a practical way to improve molecular reconstruction from IR spectra. The code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SpecCal-B18A.

</details>


### [108] [Annotating Topical Legal Insights from Case Proceedings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27792)
*Subinay Adhikary,Dwaipayan Roy,Debasis Ganguly,Shouvik Kumar Guha,Kripabandhu Ghosh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LeDA系统，实现对法律案件文本的概念标注与仲裁，支持动态创建标签，已用于印度最高法院案例的概念标注，可为检索、判决预测等下游任务提供语义表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的词袋模型难以捕捉法律文档的结构化信息，缺乏预定义本体时难以进行有效标注；因此需要一种灵活的概念标注工具，以构建语义化的文档表示，提升法律文本处理能力。

Method: 设计并实现LeDA（Legal Data Annotation）系统，提供基于网页的实体/概念标注与仲裁功能；核心创新在于支持标注过程中动态创建新标签，允许标注者在无预定义本体的情况下自行发现并添加概念。系统通过多评审者协作完成概念名称的标注与一致性 adjudication。

Result: LeDA已被3名评审者用于标注印度最高法院判例中的概念，成功构建了概念袋表示；实验表明该表示可用于先例检索、判决预测等任务，提升了下游模型的效果。

Conclusion: LeDA提供了一种灵活、可扩展的法律文档概念标注平台，解决了缺乏本体的标注难题，并验证了其在实际法律案例中的可行性和对后续法律智能任务的价值。

Abstract: In this paper, we mainly concentrate on finding concepts or topics from the legal case proceedings, since adopting a structured representation for legal documents, as opposed to a mere bag-of-words flat text representation, can significantly enhance processing capabilities. To achieve this objective, we put forward a set of diverse concepts for legal case proceedings. With this motivation, we propose LeDA, a system for Legal Data Annotation. The system offers the generic functionality of annotating and adjudicating entities or concepts within documents via a web-based interface. A novel feature of our system is that it allows to dynamic create new tags for annotation, which is a particularly useful provision for situations where there exists no pre-defined ontology for the entities (concepts) that need to be annotated - these being rather discovered by annotators as they continue examining more documents. The system that we demonstrate is currently in use to annotate a set of concepts from legal documents to construct semantic representations of documents as bags of concepts that can then be used for several downstream tasks, such as prior case retrieval, judgment prediction, and so on. Along with the system features in general, we also describe how LeDA was used by 3 assessors to annotate and adjudicate legal concept names from Indian Supreme Court case proceedings.

</details>


### [109] [MemeBench: What LVLMs Miss When Interpreting Culture-Dependent Memes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27798)
*Weihang Wang,Kainan Tu,Jielei Zhang,Run Yang,Boheng Sheng,Yuchen He,Yu Xie,Pengyu Chen,Peiyi Li,Huyang Sun,Longwen Gao,Zhouhui Lian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MemeBench 是一个包含 1,253 条中英双语 meme 的诊断基准，使用 VIKR（视觉、身份、知识、推理）框架细分解释要素。实验表明 26 种大视觉语言模型在视觉覆盖上表现好于知识覆盖，仍存在约 22.6% 的视觉‑知识差距。基于实体检索的 KAR 方法可提升 VIKR 成功率 3.6‑7.4%，尤其改善身份和知识部分，但会略削弱视觉覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型虽能生成准确的视觉描述，但在需要超出像素信息的文化、背景知识时常出现解释缺失。meme 作为高度依赖文化实体和社区约定的文本-图像混合体，凸显了模型在知识推理上的不足，而现有 meme 基准往往只给出整体标签或评分，无法定位解释失效的具体环节。

Method: 构建 MemeBench：收集 1,253 条中英 meme，围绕动漫、漫画、游戏等亚文化，提供人工撰写的参考解释并采用 VIKR 注释方案将解释拆解为 Visual（视觉线索）、Identity（身份关联）、Knowledge（知识单元）和 Reasoning（推理机制）。在 26 种大视觉语言模型上评估 VIKR 各要素的覆盖率。随后提出 KAR（Knowledge‑Augmented Retrieval）基线，利用 CultureBase 实体库进行检索，引入实体级证据以辅助模型生成解释，并与通用检索进行对比。

Result: 实验发现所有模型在视觉线索覆盖上均优于知识需求，平均仍有 22.6% 的视觉‑知识差距。KAR 在四个受控模型上将 VIKR 成功率提升 3.6%‑7.4%，相较于普通检索能修复更多错误答案且破坏更少。两种检索方式均提升 Identity 与 Knowledge 的覆盖，但均导致 Visual 覆盖略有下降。

Conclusion: MemeBench 提供了细粒度的解释诊断工具，能够明确模型在视觉、身份、知识和推理四个维度的不足。针对性地引入实体检索（如 KAR）能够弥补部分知识缺口，提升解释质量，但仍需平衡视觉信息的保持。该基准可用于评估和指导未来大视觉语言模型在跨文化、跨知识场景下的解释能力提升。

Abstract: Large vision-language models have improved at describing visual content, but accurate descriptions do not ensure interpretation when meaning depends on knowledge beyond the pixels. Memes expose this gap because they rely on cultural entities, background knowledge, and community conventions. Most meme benchmarks reduce interpretation to labels or holistic scores, obscuring where an explanation breaks down. We introduce MemeBench, a diagnostic benchmark of 1,253 Chinese and English memes with human-written references and quality-controlled VIKR annotations, centered on anime, comics, games, and adjacent online subcultures. Its VIKR schema decomposes explanations into Visual clues, Identity links, Knowledge units, and Reasoning mechanisms. Across 26 LVLMs, every model covers visible content more reliably than the knowledge needed to interpret it, and even the strongest retains a 22.6% Visual-Knowledge gap. To test whether this diagnosis can guide improvement, we introduce KAR, an entity-guided retrieval baseline built on CultureBase. Across four controlled models, KAR raises VIKR Success by 3.6-7.4% and, compared with generic retrieval, repairs more answers and breaks fewer. Yet both retrieval conditions improve Identity and Knowledge while reducing Visual coverage in every comparison. MemeBench reveals whether an interpretation succeeds, what is missing, and whether targeted evidence fills the diagnosed gap.

</details>


### [110] [STEREODISCO: Discovering Stereotypicality in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27824)
*Farane Jalali Farahani,Corina Dima,Mojtaba Nayyeri,Raphael H. Heiberger,Steffen Staab*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 STEREODISCO 框架，通过 WordNet 反义词构建约 2000 条语义轴并在 LLM 激活空间中探测，发现 LLM（LLAMA‑3‑8B‑INSTRUCT、MISTRAL‑7B‑INSTRUCT）中存在大量未被先前研究关注的刻板印象轴，且模型的刻板印象与人类心理学数据差异更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究仅在少数社会心理学定义的语义维度上检视 LLM 的刻板印象，并且主要基于词向量，未系统探索 LLM 内部可能携带的其他刻板印象维度及其表征方式。

Method: STEREODISCO 将 Osgood 的语义差分法引入 LLM 表征分析：① 从 WordNet 反义词同义词集生成约 2000 条候选语义轴；② 通过探针模型在 LLM 的激活空间中恢复每条轴的几何方向；③ 对概念在这些轴上的投影进行统计检验，以识别显著的刻板印象轴。

Result: 在对 LLAMA‑3‑8B‑INSTRUCT 与 MISTRAL‑7B‑INSTRUCT 的实验中：① 两模型在社会群体评分上相互一致度高于与人类评分的吻合度，表明模型内部的刻板印象与社会心理学记录存在偏差；② 发现多条先前未被关注的刻板印象轴，如 humble‑proud、narrow‑minded‑broad‑minded、cowardly‑brave，且这些轴经独立人工标注验证有效。

Conclusion: STEREODISCO 能系统揭示 LLM 中隐藏的多维刻板印象，表明 LLM 编码的刻板印象内容与人类社会心理学的描述并不完全一致，提示需进一步审视和调控模型内部的偏见表征。

Abstract: LLMs encode, convey, and perpetuate stereotypes. Prior computational research focuses on a small set of semantic axes investigated in social psychology, and operates on word embeddings produced by language models, leaving open which other semantic axes carry stereotypical associations in LLMs and how LLMs internally represent such axes. We introduce STEREODISCO, a framework that adapts the semantic differential method (Osgood et al., 1957) to the systematic study of stereotypes in LLM internal representations. STEREODISCO constructs approx. 2,000 candidate semantic axes from WordNet antonym synsets, recovers each as a geometric axis in the LLM's activation space via probing, and identifies stereotypical axes via a statistical test over concept projections. As a case study, we apply STEREODISCO to social group stereotypes with LLAMA-3-8B-INSTRUCT and MISTRAL-7B-INSTRUCT. We find that the two LLMs agree with each other on social group ratings more than with humans, suggesting that LLM-encoded stereotype content diverges from that documented in social psychology. We also discover stereotypical axes not investigated in prior work -- including humble vs. proud, narrow-minded vs. broad-minded, and cowardly vs. brave, which human annotators independently confirm.

</details>


### [111] [Sign Language Question Answering: A New Task, Benchmark, and Baseline for Sign Language Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27826)
*Shiwei Gan,Lichen Wang,Xiao Liu,Yafeng Yin,Kuizhuang Liu,Sanglu Lu,Lei Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了手语问答（SLQA）任务及两套基于PHOENIX14T和CSL-Daily的SignQA基准数据，设计了问题条件调制的时间下采样模块和领域知识迁移策略，显著提升了对手语视频的语义理解评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手语识别和翻译任务只能评估固定映射，无法全面衡量模型对手语视频语义的理解能力，亟需更灵活、综合的评估方式。

Method: 构建SLQA任务，自动从手语词汇和句子标注生成五类问题（位置、结构、视觉检索、词汇识别、翻译理解），并提出基线模型：包括Question‑Conditioned Modulated Temporal Downsampling（Q‑CMTD）模块用于问题感知的时序特征建模，以及基于已有SLU任务的领域知识迁移策略。

Result: 在两套SignQA数据集上，所提基线模型在所有五类问题上均显著优于主流视觉‑语言模型，验证了模块设计和知识迁移的有效性。

Conclusion: SLQA提供了评估手语理解的更全面框架，基线模型设立了强基准，推动后续研究在手语语义推理和跨模态学习方面的发展。

Abstract: Recent advances in sign language (SL) understanding (SLU) have led to remarkable progress in tasks such as continuous SL recognition and SL translation. However, these tasks are designed with predefined objectives, requiring models to learn a fixed mapping from sign videos to glosses or spoken-language sentences. As a result, they provide only a limited assessment of whether a model truly understands the semantic content of SL videos. To address this limitation, \textbf{we first propose a new task, Sign Language Question Answering (SLQA)}, which evaluates SL understanding by requiring models to answer arbitrary natural language questions about SL videos. Unlike previous SLU tasks, SLQA provides a more flexible and comprehensive evaluation framework that assesses multiple reasoning capabilities beyond recognition and translation. To facilitate this task, \textbf{we further construct two SignQA benchmarks} based on PHOENIX14T and CSL-Daily by automatically generating question-answer pairs from existing gloss and sentence annotations using carefully designed templates. The resulting datasets cover five complementary question categories, including position reasoning, structural reasoning, visual search, gloss recognition, and translation understanding. \textbf{Finally, we propose a simple yet effective baseline model} equipped with a Question-Conditioned Modulated Temporal Downsampling module and an in-domain knowledge transfer strategy, enabling effective knowledge transfer from existing SLU tasks while enhancing question-aware temporal feature modeling. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our baseline consistently outperforms representative vision-language models across all question categories, establishing a strong benchmark for future research on SLQA. Datasets are available at:{https://huggingface.co/datasets/hulala/SignQA-2026}.

</details>


### [112] [MemTxn: A Transaction Boundary for Source-Supported Updates and Complete-State Recovery in Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27834)
*Hanshuai Cui,Zhiqing Tang,Zhi Yao,Fanshuai Meng,Qianli Ma,Weijia Jia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MemTxn 在持久化记忆中提供事务边界，确保更新可靠并在故障后恢复正确状态，显著提升 LLM 代理的记忆一致性和答案质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久化记忆使 LLM 代理能够跨会话复用信息，但写入错误会持久化并导致后续行为错误，现有系统缺乏事务机制来保证更新的可靠性和可恢复性。

Method: 提出 MemTxn 作为答案模型之外的治理层，采用 Ordered PatchTest 验证写入合法性，使用 Temporal Resolver 在冲突时选择可见版本，并通过持久快照日志实现状态恢复。系统在不知晓实际物理写集合的情况下恢复完整的活动映射。

Result: 在 item‑disjoint audit 中，MemTxn 接受全部 60 条支持的原始更新，拒绝全部 179 条硬负例。面对 LongMemEval‑S 与 LoCoMo 的多键持久故障，成功恢复完整声明的活动映射。MemoryAgentBench FactConsolidation 中，MemTxn 在 12 种答案模型配置下的平均 F1 最高，较 Dense 方法提升 17.06–24.07 分。

Conclusion: MemTxn 为持久化记忆提供了可靠的事务边界和恢复机制，显著提升了 LLM 代理的记忆一致性和答案质量，证明在实际多任务场景中具备实用价值。

Abstract: Persistent memory lets long-running large language model agents reuse information across sessions and tasks. Yet errors in writable memory can persist and corrupt future behavior. Existing systems improve storage and retrieval, but they do not provide a transaction boundary for reliable updates and recovery. We therefore propose MemTxn, a governance layer outside the answer model. MemTxn verifies whether an update is supported by its source. It also selects the visible version when facts conflict and restores the application-visible state after a fault. The system uses Ordered PatchTest to validate writes, a Temporal Resolver to select versions, and a durable snapshot journal to recover state. On an item-disjoint audit, MemTxn accepts all 60 supported originals and rejects all 179 hard negatives. Under persistent multi-key faults on LongMemEval-S and LoCoMo states, it restores the complete declared active map without knowing the actual physical write set. On MemoryAgentBench FactConsolidation, MemTxn achieves the highest average F1 across all twelve answer-model configurations. It outperforms Dense by 17.06--24.07 points in five representative settings.

</details>


### [113] [Crossing the Margin Cliff: Toward Relearn-Robust LLM Unlearning via Margin Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27836)
*Xiangyu Yin,Jiaxu Liu,Zhen Chen,Chih-Hong Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 大语言模型的遗忘机制在重新学习攻击下极易被恢复，作者发现多数后置遗忘方法在每个实验单元上都出现“margin cliff”现象，并提出非饱和的Margin Calibration（MC）插件，通过在参考模型的每标记margin上加hinge和KL探测，显著提升遗忘效果且在多种模型和数据集上实现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的后置遗忘技术在面对少量重新微调（relearn）时表现脆弱，导致被遗忘的内容容易被恢复。作者希望解释这种脆弱性的根本原因，并设计一种通用的改进手段，以在保持模型效用的同时增强遗忘的稳健性。

Method: 作者系统性地评估了14种后置遗忘方法（梯度、偏好、蒸馏等），发现它们的每标记答案margin在41/42的实验格子中收敛到一个狭窄的高于保留基准的区间，称为“margin cliff”。在此理论基础上，提出Margin Calibration（MC）插件：在参考模型的每标记margin上加一个非饱和的margin hinge，并在独立指令语料上加入KL探测项，以在原始损失饱和时重新施加遗忘压力。作者还给出在梯度支配条件下的收敛分析，提供攻击预算上界。

Result: 在TOFU基准（3种Llama‑3规模、3个遗忘层级）、MUSE‑News（Llama‑2‑7B）以及Phi‑3.5面板实验中，单一的冻结配置配合MC在所有14项遗忘聚合指标和所有可比较的重新学习单元上均取得最佳表现（攻击后ROUGE‑L从0.41降至0.18），并在13/14的实验中降低原始成员资格AUC，唯一的代价是保留侧效用略有下降。部署变体在无需保留参考的情况下也实现了相似增益。

Conclusion: 遗忘的脆弱性源于优化几何导致的margin cliff，非饱和的Margin Calibration能够有效突破该限制，在多模型、多任务上实现更稳健的遗忘，同时提供理论上的攻击预算上界，为实际部署提供可行的后置遗忘方案。

Abstract: Large language model unlearning is consistently fragile under relearn attacks. On TOFU, fine-tuning on twenty forget examples substantially recovers held-out forget-set ROUGE for every method we evaluate, and we trace this fragility to optimization geometry. The per-token answer margin of fourteen post-hoc methods spanning gradient, preference, and distillation families converges into a narrow band above the retain reference in 41 of 42 method--size cells, a regularity we call the margin cliff. We prove that this cliff follows whenever the retain coupling holds the diagnostic log-odds of forget content above a floor, a condition that token-saturating losses induce at stationarity and that we verify directly on 34 of 42 cells. Margin Calibration (\textsc{MC}) is a plug-in polish adding a non-saturating margin hinge anchored at the reference's per-token margin plus a KL probe on a disjoint instruction corpus, restoring forget-side pressure where the native loss saturates. Under a stated gradient-dominance condition, whose on-trajectory gradient signature we measure by instrumenting the polish, its stationary set lies on the cliff-crossing side, yielding an attack-budget upper bound on the relearn margin lift. Across TOFU (three Llama-3 sizes, three forget tiers), MUSE-News on Llama-2-7B-hf, and a Phi-3.5 panel, a single frozen configuration wins all 14 head-to-head forget aggregates and all populated relearn cells (panel-mean post-attack ROUGE-L $0.41$ to $0.18$) and lowers raw membership AUC on 13/14, with reduced retain-side utility as the main cost. A deployment variant matches these gains without a retain-trained reference.

</details>


### [114] [Virtual Process Dossier: A Process-Aware Data Catalogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27840)
*Lukas Kubelka,Alexander Bott,Frank Döhner,Saksham Kiroriwal,Georg Zeeb,Julia Butte,Julius Pfrommer,Jürgen Beyerer,Tobias Käfer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于知识图谱的虚拟流程档案（VPD），用于多阶段制造中的数据目录与工作流溯源，实现FAIR数据管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多阶段制造中，下游AI优化任务需要区分不同工作流步骤产生的数据，现有数据目录缺乏对工作流溯源的明确表示，导致数据可重用性和可追溯性不足。

Method: 构建VPD本体作为语义核心，设计VPD溯源框架将本体实例化嵌入生产环境，并实现面向用户的交互界面，以图形化方式展示和查询知识图谱中的数据与工作流信息。

Result: 实现了一个能够以FAIR原则管理数据并显式记录前向和回溯工作流溯源的系统，提供了本体、代码开源，支持在实际制造案例中区分并检索各步骤生成的数据集。

Conclusion: VPD通过本体驱动的知识图谱和溯源框架，为制造业数据管理提供了可扩展、可交互且符合FAIR标准的解决方案，促进了下游AI优化的可靠性和效率。

Abstract: We propose the Virtual Process Dossier (VPD), a Knowledge Graph-based data catalogue that also captures workflow provenance. We developed VPD for multi-stage manufacturing use-cases where downstream AI-based optimization tasks require to distinct between datasets generated during individual workflow steps. VPD provides these datasets in a FAIR manner and makes both prospective and retrospective workflow provenance explicit. Our contributions are: (1) the VPD ontology that serves as the catalogue's semantic core; (2) the VPD provenance framework that integrates ontology instantiation into the production environment; and (3) the VPD user interface that provides human-centered interaction with the VPD Knowledge Graph. The ontology and code are available at https://github.com/kubeluk/VirtualProcessDossier .

</details>


### [115] [Simplifying Neural Networks During Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27854)
*Lorenzo Sciandra,Samuele Fonio,Roberto Esposito*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于神经坍缩（NC）和逆Fisher准则的训练框架，在训练过程中动态识别特征提取层与分类层的分界点，并在合适阶段用轻量分类头替换后置层，实现显著参数削减且保持精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过参数化深度网络的训练动态难以理解；神经坍缩揭示了类表示的高度结构化几何，隧道效应表明仅部分层对特征提取关键。结合两者可在训练中简化网络，降低计算成本。

Method: 在训练期间通过逆Fisher准则（Inverse Fisher Criterion）监测表示的可变性坍缩，确定特征提取层与分类层的分割点以及简化的合适时机。随后将后置层替换为轻量分类头，继续对简化后的模型进行微调。

Result: 在MLP、VGG、ResNet等多种图像分类基准上实验，所提方法在保持与完整模型相近的准确率的同时，实现了显著的参数削减（具体削减比例在实验中给出），验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 利用神经坍缩的几何特性和逆Fisher准则的稳定性，可在训练过程中动态简化深度网络，实现参数和计算资源的高效利用，而不显著牺牲模型性能。

Abstract: Understanding and exploiting the training dynamics of overparameterized deep neural networks remains a central challenge in modern machine learning. Recent evidence on Neural Collapse (NC) shows that class representations and classifiers exhibit highly structured geometry, while the Tunnel Effect suggests that only a subset of layers is essential for feature extraction. We combine these two perspectives and propose an NC-inspired training framework for simplifying deep networks during training. Our method monitors representation dynamics through the Inverse Fisher Criterion, a stable and efficient proxy for the variability collapse behavior, to identify both the split point between feature extraction and classification and the training stage at which simplification becomes viable. We then replace the trailing layers with a lightweight classification head and continue training the reduced model. Experiments on image-classification benchmarks across MLP, VGG, and ResNet architectures show that the proposed method achieves substantial parameter reductions while maintaining accuracy comparable to that of the full model. Code to reproduce the experiments can be found at: https://github.com/LorenzoSciandra/NNS.

</details>


### [116] [Back to All-Entity Ranking: Sampler-Dependent Evaluation in Continuous-Time Dynamic Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27861)
*Minwoo Yu,Young-guk Ha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在连续时间动态图的下一目的地预测中，负样本的分布和候选集大小会显著影响模型排名和模块效果；全实体排名消除这些影响，建议作为主要评估方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的CTDG评估通常采用负样本抽样并在有限候选集上进行排名，这导致评估结果受负样本分布和候选数量的影响，可能误导模型优劣和模块贡献的判断。

Method: 作者分析了负样本分布对贝叶斯最优排名的影响，构建了一个仅基于源‑目的历史成员关系的最小评分器，并在六种模型上进行因子化实验，比较了Uniform-20抽样指标与全目录（all-entity）排名的差异，使用了LastFM、MOOC、Reddit、Wikipedia四个数据集。

Result: 实验发现，在三/四个数据集上，至少有一对模型在Uniform-20指标与全目录排名之间出现相对顺序变化；同一模块的效果随候选集大小和训练目标的不同而在幅度和方向上均会改变。

Conclusion: 负样本抽样会导致模型比较和消融实验的结论不稳定。全实体排名消除了负样本选择的自由度和采样波动，保留原始CTDG评分器，因而被推荐为CTDG基准上架构比较的主要评估方式。

Abstract: Next-destination prediction in continuous-time dynamic graphs (CTDGs) commonly ranks an observed interaction against sampled negative destinations. The resulting score is conditional on both the negative distribution and the number of candidates chosen by the researcher. We show that a non-uniform negative distribution changes the Bayes-optimal ranking, while even a finite candidate set drawn uniformly can destabilize model rankings and measured module effects.
  Time-varying source-destination history membership and model operations that use this information directly transmit the sampler's influence to the evaluation score. We examine this mechanism using a factorial evaluation of repeated and new positives against seen and unseen negatives, a minimal scorer based solely on pair-history membership, and controlled representation interventions. Across six models on LastFM, MOOC, Reddit, and Wikipedia, at least one model pair changes relative order between the expected Uniform-20 metric and the full catalog on three of the four datasets. The measured effect of the same module also changes in magnitude and direction with the candidate-set size and training objective.
  These results establish that model-superiority and ablation conclusions from sampled-negative benchmarks are conditional on the stated candidate configuration. All-entity ranking evaluates every destination in a fixed catalog, eliminating negative-selection freedom and sampling variation while retaining the original CTDG scorer. We therefore recommend all-entity ranking as the primary evidence for architecture comparisons on CTDG benchmarks with an enumerable, fixed destination catalog.

</details>


### [117] [Orca: Neural Operators for Causal Reasoning in Continuous Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27867)
*Gerrit Großmann,David A. Selby,Sebastian J. Vollmer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Orca框架，将因果图的节点视为随时间变化的函数，利用神经算子学习实现连续时间因果推断与反事实推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统结构因果模型只能处理一次性静态变量且不支持循环依赖，难以描述患者、气候、经济等随时间演化且存在反馈的系统。需要一种能够在不规则观测、连续时间和反馈回路下进行因果推理的方法。

Method: 将每个因果节点建模为时间函数，机制为神经算子在函数空间之间的映射。扩展现有神经算子结构，使其能够接受多个父节点函数作为输入、遵循时间箭头，并将潜在外生噪声建模为可推断的函数，以供反事实计算使用。提出的Orca框架 formalizes 该模型类并实现相应的学习与推断算法。

Result: 在合成的连续时间案例中，Orca 能够成功学习因果机制并进行准确的反事实推理，展示了对时间不规则采样和反馈环的处理能力。

Conclusion: 神经算子学习为连续时间因果推理提供了自然的实现途径，Orca 框架证明了在函数空间上建模因果机制的可行性，为处理实际中随时间演化且含反馈的系统奠定了基础。

Abstract: Structural causal models are the standard language for reasoning about interventions and counterfactuals, but they describe static variables, typically measured once, and usually forbid cyclic dependencies. Many systems we care about, such as patients, climates, and economies, instead evolve continuously in time, are observed at irregular time points, and contain feedback loops. We argue that neural operator learning provides a natural foundation for causal reasoning in this setting, and propose Orca, a framework in which each node of the causal graph is a function of time and each mechanism is a learned map between function spaces. We extend existing neural operator architectures to express causal mechanisms: a mechanism computes the function value of a node from its parent nodes by taking several parent functions as input, respects the arrow of time, and treats latent exogenous noise as a function that can be inferred and reused for counterfactuals. We formalize the model class and demonstrate counterfactual reasoning on synthetic continuous-time examples. Code is available at https://github.com/gerritgr/orca

</details>


### [118] [Search as Computation Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27871)
*Alexander Tuisov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将需要内部资源的算法形式化为终端计算分配问题，利用贝尔曼方程求解在预算、计算费用和精确认证约束下的最优资源分配，并将计算价值（VOC）与信息量关联，展示在不同计算拓扑（如Bandit、树搜索）中的应用，最终证明在近似VOC最大化下可恢复加权A*搜索，A*和贪婪最佳优先搜索是其极限情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多算法在返回最终决策前会进行昂贵的内部计算，但评估仅基于最终输出的质量，缺乏对计算资源分配的系统化理论。作者希望建立统一的框架，解释如何在有限预算或计算费用下最优地分配这些内部计算，以提升决策质量。

Method: 作者将此类算法抽象为“终端计算‑分配问题”，其中计算产生观测、更新对潜在环境的信念，并通过终端决策损失体现价值。利用贝尔曼方程推导在固定预算、计价计算和精确认证三种情形下的最优分配策略。进一步，将计算价值（VOC）与信息论量关联：在对数损失下，VOC等价于互信息的短视近似；在简单后悔（simple regret）下，VOC对应知识梯度（knowledge‑gradient）形式。通过带宽、树模拟、节点展开等不同计算拓扑展示模型的通用性。最后，在显式的前沿分辨率和启发式误差模型下，近似最大化VOC可得到加权A*搜索，A*和贪婪最佳优先搜索分别是其极端情况。

Result: 理论上证明：① 贝尔曼方程能够描述在多种约束下的最优计算分配；② VOC 与信息量的关系揭示了互信息并非总是良好的计算价值度量，但可提供单向上界；③ 在不同计算拓扑中，模型统一解释了Bandit 拉取、树搜索模拟和节点扩展的最优策略；④ 在前沿‑启发式模型中，最大化近似VOC自然导出加权A*，并在参数极限下回归到传统的A*和贪婪最佳优先搜索。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个统一的终端计算分配理论，说明在资源受限或计价计算情境下，最优的计算分配策略可以通过贝尔曼方程和VOC 最大化来确定。该框架既解释了现有的多种搜索与决策算法，又表明没有单一的获取规则在所有情形下都是最优的。

Abstract: Many algorithms spend an internal resource before returning a decision and are evaluated only by the quality of that terminal output. We formalize such procedures as terminal computation-allocation problems: costly computations produce observations, update beliefs about a latent environment, and matter only through terminal decision loss. Bellman equations characterize optimal allocation under fixed budgets, priced computation, and exact certification. We then relate value of computation (VOC) to information. Mutual information equals myopic VOC under log loss, whereas under simple regret VOC is a knowledge-gradient quantity; moreover, information gain can rank computations arbitrarily poorly, although it gives a one-sided upper bound on VOC. Bandit pulls, tree simulations, and node expansions illustrate the same model under different computation topologies. Finally, under an explicit frontier-resolution and heuristic-error model, maximizing approximate VOC recovers weighted A*, with A* and greedy best-first search as limiting cases. The theory identifies a shared decision problem without asserting that one acquisition rule is universally optimal.

</details>


### [119] [An Empirical Study of Coordination Mode as the First-Class Citizen in From-Scratch Multi-Agent Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27877)
*Yanyu Ren,Yunfeng Bai,Xizheng Wang,Li Chen,Dan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出真实全栈项目的多智能体编码基准 MSEval，评估不同协作拓扑对软件开发速度、成本和质量的影响，发现组织结构对性能影响显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体编码基准多基于合成环境，忽视实际时间、金钱成本，混淆推理与沟通，仅奖励表面完成，难以衡量真实软件开发中的效率与质量。

Method: 构建 MSEval 基准，选取 10 个跨 10 个领域的真实全栈项目，使用分层需求和确定性评分标准。搭建执行引擎 LegoGent，测试 10 种协作拓扑（周期同步、CI/CD 部署），并通过自动评分器 TAgent 动态探测实现，综合衡量功能成功率、延迟和前缀缓存 token 成本，进行 100 次实验。

Result: 实验表明，在相同任务和模型下，不同协作拓扑可导致分数相差 30 分以上，墙钟时间翻倍。结构化流水线实现最快收敛且质量最高，而过度管理的拓扑则显著降低性能。

Conclusion: MSEval 为多智能体团队实际构建软件提供了严格、可复现的评估标准，证明组织拓扑是影响速度、成本、质量权衡的关键因素。

Abstract: Multi-agent vibe coding promises to accelerate software development, yet existing benchmarks rely on synthetic environments that ignore practical time and monetary costs, conflate reasoning with communication, and reward only superficial completion. We introduce multi-agent from-scratch evaluation benchmark, MSEval, evaluating multi-agent coding on real-world tasks. Grounded in 10 authentic, full-stack projects across 10 domains, MSEval scores performance using hierarchical requirements and deterministic rubrics.
  Its execution engine, LegoGent, tests 10 collaboration topologies where agents coordinate via periodic sync intervals and deploy through native CI/CD pipelines. Concurrently, the automated grader TAgent dynamically probes implementations to jointly measure functional success, latency, and prefix-cached token cost. Across 100 runs, MSEval reveals that organizational topology rivals model capability in shaping the speed--cost--quality trade-off. For identical tasks and models, varying the topology shifts scores by over 30 points and doubles wall-clock time. Structured pipelines converge fastest with the highest quality, whereas heavy managerial oversight degrades performance. Ultimately, MSEval establishes a rigorous, reproducible standard for measuring how multi-agent teams actually build software. The benchmark is released at https://github.com/robinren03/MSEval.

</details>


### [120] [Not All Tokens Deserve Equal Credit: Counterfactual Sensitivity Credit Reallocation for Long-CoT Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27888)
*Qiangqiang He,Zhongheng Wu,ZiJian Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 作者发现现有的RLVR方法（如GRPO）在对每个token分配奖励时忽视了其对答案的真实贡献，而OPSD的特权策略产生的梯度方向并不可靠。基于此提出CSCR，通过降低对高反事实敏感token的奖励并重新归一化，实现了在长CoT数学推理任务上相较GRPO的稳健提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大语言模型的长链思考（long‑CoT）中，如何利用可验证奖励进行强化学习仍是关键挑战。现有的无批评者方法（GRPO）对所有token统一分配优势，忽略了它们对最终答案的不同贡献；而自蒸馏方法（OPSD）假设特权策略的概率迁移能提供可靠的答案对齐信息，但该假设缺乏验证。

Method: 作者固定采样轨迹，分别在“正确”和“错误”两种对立结果下重新打分，分析token级别的优势变化。发现大多数token在两种条件下的方向相同，且变化幅度集中在可替换的表层token上。基于此提出Counterfactual Sensitivity Credit Reallocation (CSCR)：① 计算每个token的反事实敏感度；② 对高敏感度token的优势进行下调；③ 对剩余优势重新归一化，保持整体信用预算不变，同时保留验证器给出的方向信息。

Result: 在多个长CoT数学推理基准上，CSCR在相同的策略更新次数下 consistently 超过 GRPO 基线。消融实验表明：① 特权策略产生的方向不可靠；② 适度的下权重最有效；③ 过强的调制会导致优化不稳定。

Conclusion: 特权策略的梯度方向并非答案对齐的可靠信号，其幅度更多反映反事实敏感性而非学习价值。通过对高敏感度token进行信用重新分配，CSCR 能在保持原有奖励方向的前提下提升长CoT 推理的效果，为强化学习在大模型可验证奖励中的应用提供了新的诊断与改进思路。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) is central to improving long-CoT reasoning in large language models. Critic-free methods such as GRPO convert response-level rewards into advantages and uniformly broadcast them across tokens, overlooking their unequal contributions to the final outcome. On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) instead provides dense distributional supervision by minimizing the forward KL divergence between an unprivileged policy and a privileged self-teacher, implicitly assuming that the resulting likelihood shifts encode reliable answer-aligned information. We test this premise by fixing each sampled trajectory and re-scoring it under two opposing outcome conditions, one asserting correctness and the other incorrectness. Most affected tokens shift in the same direction under both conditions, with few sign reversals and substantial overlap in the induced optimization signals. Large shifts also concentrate on highly substitutable surface-form tokens, whereas tokens carrying problem-specific reasoning content are less sensitive. These findings show that privileged shifts fail to provide reliable answer-aligned directions, while their magnitudes primarily reflect counterfactual sensitivity rather than token-level learning value. Based on these observations, we propose Counterfactual Sensitivity Credit Reallocation (CSCR), a simple extension of GRPO that reduces credit for highly sensitive tokens and renormalizes token-level advantages to preserve both the original credit budget and verifier-determined direction. On long-CoT mathematical reasoning benchmarks, CSCR consistently outperforms GRPO baseline with the same number of policy updates. Targeted ablations further corroborate our diagnosis: privilege-induced directions are unreliable, moderate downweighting is most effective, and stronger modulation destabilizes optimization.

</details>


### [121] [Dynamic Spectral Filtering for Temporal Graph Learning: Learning Evolving Propagation Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27891)
*Yan Kong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出动态谱过滤（DSF）方法，通过时间依赖的Chebyshev多项式系数实现图传播机制随时间演化，在多个时序链接预测数据集上实现高精度且显著降低参数量、显存和训练时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时序图学习主要关注节点状态或交互历史的演化，忽视了图传播机制本身随时间变化的可能性。若传播算子能够动态适应时间，则有望在保持或提升预测性能的同时提升计算效率。

Method: DSF 将每个快照 t 的图传播表示为带有向量化、时间依赖系数的 Chebyshev 多项式滤波器。将这些紧凑的多阶系数视为循环的时间状态；循环分支负责生成系数更新，乘法全局门和阶特定门调节系数幅度。该时间状态的维度与节点数无关，从而实现高效的参数共享。

Result: 在 MOOC、Wikipedia、Reddit 三个时序链接预测基准上，DSF 达到 AP 分别为 0.7851、0.9088、0.9860，模型参数仅 93K~133K，显存峰值 68~182 MB，单 epoch 训练时长 1.6~2.1 秒。相较于 DEFT，DSF 在 MOOC 上更好，Reddit 上相差 0.001，Wikipedia 上略低；但参数量减少 8.3~8.6 倍，显存降低 25~33 倍，训练时间缩短 5~19 倍。相对所有对比方法，显存使用降低 3.3~38.6 倍。

Conclusion: 直接对谱响应进行时间演化是一种有效的时序归纳偏置，能够在保证预测性能的前提下显著提升计算和存储效率，适用于对资源受限的时序图学习任务。

Abstract: Temporal graph learning is commonly organized around the evolution of node states or the encoding of interaction histories. We study an underexplored, operator-centric question: should the graph propagation mechanism itself evolve over time? We introduce Dynamic Spectral Filtering (DSF), which represents propagation at snapshot t by a Chebyshev polynomial filter with vector-valued, time-dependent coefficients. DSF explicitly treats these compact multi-order coefficients as recurrent temporal states. A recurrent branch proposes updates, while multiplicative global and order-specific gates regulate their magnitude. The temporal state is independent of the number of nodes. On MOOC, Wikipedia, and Reddit temporal link-prediction benchmarks, converged DSF runs attain AP scores of 0.7851, 0.9088, and 0.9860, respectively, with 93K to 133K trainable parameters, 68 to 182 MB peak GPU memory, and 1.6 to 2.1 seconds of training per epoch. Against the closely related DEFT baseline, DSF is better on MOOC, within 0.001 AP on Reddit, and modestly lower on Wikipedia, while using 8.3 to 8.6 times fewer parameters, 25 to 33 times less GPU memory, and 5 to 19 times less time per epoch. Relative to all measured alternatives, it uses 3.3 to 38.6 times less GPU memory. These results support direct spectral-response evolution as a useful temporal inductive bias when computational efficiency is a first-class requirement.

</details>


### [122] [MMHBench: A Multi-Perspective Benchmark for Mental Health Understanding in Long-Form Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27895)
*Jinpeng Hu,Erqiang Wang,Shan Wang,Zhuo Li,Peipei Song,Xun Yang,Meng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MMHBench，一个包含268段长视频、2184问的多模态心理健康理解基准，分为第三人称行为解读和第一人称视角推理两大设置，并用多角色问生成框架MAQG生成高质量问题；实验表明22种多模态大模型在此任务上仍表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有心理健康评估基准过于粗粒度，仅做分类，难以检验模型是否真正理解行为、情境和潜在心理状态，亟需更细致、多视角的长视频评估平台。

Method: 构建MMHBench：收集268段长视频，设计2,184个经专家筛选的问题，分为第三人称行为解释（605问）和第一人称视角推理（1,579问）。提出多代理问生成框架MAQG，模拟不同社会角色生成问题，利用多角色反馈和迭代优化提升问题质量，最终由专家验证。

Result: 在MMHBench上评测22个代表性多模态大语言模型（包括开源和闭源），结果显示这些模型在长视频心理健康理解任务上仍然困难，整体表现远低于人类水平。

Conclusion: MMHBench提供了更具挑战性和细粒度的心理健康理解评估，揭示当前多模态大模型在深层心理推理方面的局限，为未来模型改进指明方向。

Abstract: Mental health understanding in long-form videos requires nuanced reasoning over observable behavior, interpersonal context, and latent psychological states. Existing benchmarks largely reduce this task to coarse-grained classification, providing limited insight into whether models truly understand psychological phenomena or rely on superficial correlations. To address this limitation, we introduce MMHBench, a comprehensive multimodal benchmark for multi-perspective mental health understanding, comprising 268 long-form videos and 2,184 carefully curated questions. MMHBench organizes the evaluation into two complementary settings: (1) third-person assessment, consisting of 605 questions that focus on the interpretation of observable behaviors and multimodal evidence, and (2) first-person perspective-taking, comprising 1,579 questions that require perspective-conditioned reasoning to identify the interpretation of the mental state supported by the available multimodal evidence. We propose a Multi-Agent Question Generation (MAQG) framework that simulates diverse social roles to synthesize questions from multiple perspectives. The generated questions are refined through multi-role feedback and iterative optimization, followed by expert-guided verification to ensure quality and validity. Extensive evaluation of 22 representative multimodal large language models (MLLMs), spanning both open-source and leading closed-source models, demonstrates that long-form video mental health understanding remains highly challenging.

</details>


### [123] [A Cross-Architecture Audit of Direction-Based Inference-Time Defences in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27910)
*Xiangyu Yin,Tora Bodin,Rohan Menon,Chih-Hong Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 对视觉语言模型的推理时防御方法进行系统比较，发现没有单一方案在拒绝恢复和实用性保持上均优，图像条件偏移在多数模型上表现最佳，但防御方向需针对每个解码器家族单独校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着视觉语言模型（VLM）易受 jailbreak 攻击，现有的推理时防御通过在解码层残差流中减去校准方向来抑制攻击，但缺乏系统评估不同防御方向的有效性和通用性。

Method: 在四类模型架构的 15 种模型‑层组合上，采用幅度受控协议，对五种防御候选（平均图像条件偏移、CMRM 拒绝方向、ShiftDC 攻击特定残差、忽略图像的指令、随机控制）进行比较；每个提示的干预大小相同，并为每个方向配对等范数的随机向量进行对照。

Result: 没有单一候选在拒绝恢复和实用性保持上同时占优。图像条件偏移在 LLaVA 1.5 与 Pixtral 12B 上表现最佳，且在所有家族中实用性损失仅在测量噪声水平；指令方式在 Qwen2.5 VL 上领先，攻击特定残差在 Qwen2 VL 2B 上领先。图像条件方向在 13/15 细胞中具方向特异性，但强烈受架构影响，跨 LLaVA 1.5 13B 与 Pixtral 12B 之外不可迁移。CMRM 方向与图像条件偏移的余弦相似度为 0.35（范围 0.17‑0.65），显著高于随机向量，统计显著 (p≈3e-5)。

Conclusion: 两种防御配方恢复的几何空间部分重叠，表明基于方向的防御必须针对每个语言解码器家族单独校准，且不存在通用的最优防御方案。

Abstract: Inference time defences against vision language model jailbreaks often subtract a calibrated direction from the residual stream at a chosen decoder layer. We compare five defence candidates across 15 model and layer cells from four architectural families under a magnitude controlled protocol that matches the intervention size for each prompt and pairs every direction with a random control of the same norm. The candidates are the mean image conditioning shift, a CMRM style refusal direction, a ShiftDC style attack specific residual, a prompt instruction to ignore the image, and a random control. No single candidate dominates on both refusal recovery and utility preservation. The image conditioning shift leads on LLaVA 1.5 and Pixtral 12B and is the only candidate whose utility loss remains at the measurement noise floor in every family. The prompt instruction leads on Qwen2.5 VL, while the attack specific residual leads on Qwen2 VL 2B. The image conditioning direction is direction specific in 13 of 15 cells, but strongly architecture specific and nontransferable across the only dimension compatible pair, LLaVA 1.5 13B and Pixtral 12B. We also connect text only and multimodal refusal geometry. The CMRM direction has positive cosine alignment with the image conditioning shift in all 15 cells, with mean 0.35, range 0.17 to 0.65, 15 to 25 times the random vector null, and a sign test p value of about 3e-5. These results show that the two recipes recover partially overlapping geometry and that direction based defences should be calibrated separately for each language decoder family.

</details>


### [124] [IFHierBench: Hierarchical Instruction Following for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27912)
*Yuetian Mao,Chunyang Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: IFHierBench是一套层次化指令遵循基准，包含600个不同深度的约束任务，评估发现即使是最强模型在满足嵌套约束方面也仅略超50%，表现随约束层级加深急剧下降，表明当前LLM在层次约束遵循上仍有显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际应用中，LLM需要在单次调用中生成满足多层次、结构化约束的输出，而现有基准只把约束视为平铺的列表，无法评估模型对特定输出层级的约束遵循能力，导致缺乏对真实场景的衡量。

Method: 构建IFHierBench：600条提示，覆盖四种约束树深度和35种不同约束类型；为每条提示配备确定性检查器，能够在整体、章节、字段等不同作用域上验证约束满足情况。随后在七个主流闭源和开源模型上进行评测，统计每个提示的准确率并分析随深度的变化趋势。

Result: 评测显示，最强模型的提示级准确率仅略高于50%；随着约束树深度增加，准确率显著下降，尤其在深层嵌套约束上表现尤为差劲。不同模型之间差距有限，整体上均未能可靠满足层次化约束。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在遵循层次化、嵌套约束方面仍存在显著缺口，亟需研发考虑约束细粒度的训练或微调方法，以提升真实任务中的指令遵循能力。

Abstract: Instruction-following ability is critical for deploying large language models in real-world applications, where downstream components depend on the output satisfying specific constraints. Modern deployments increasingly handle the full task in a single LLM call, with one prompt specifying a layered output whose overall artifact, structural sections, and nested fields must each satisfy concrete constraints. Existing instruction-following benchmarks treat the constraint set as a flat list applied uniformly to the response, so they cannot scope a check to a particular section of the output. We introduce IFHierBench, a hierarchical instruction-following benchmark of 600 prompts stratified across four constraint-tree depths and 35 distinct constraints, each prompt paired with a deterministic checker that verifies satisfaction at every scope. Evaluating seven leading proprietary and open-weight models, we find that even the strongest model only marginally exceeds 50% prompt-level accuracy and that accuracy degrades sharply as constraint depth grows. Reliably following nested constraints remains a substantial gap for current LLMs, motivating future training methods that consider constraint adherence at finer granularity to achieve better instruction-following ability.

</details>


### [125] [One Anchor for All: Unified Multilingual and Multimodal Safety Alignment for LVLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27917)
*Enyi Shi,Fei Shen,Chuancheng Shi,Linxia Zhu,Shuyi Miao,Jinhui Tang,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于神经元层面的跨模态跨语言安全对齐框架（MLS‑Neurons），只微调约0.03%参数，即可在多语言多模态视觉语言模型中防御复合攻击，显著提升安全性能并保持通用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大规模视觉语言模型在全球范围的部署，多语言指令与视觉信息的结合使得恶意攻击更隐蔽、复杂；现有防御方法仅针对语言或模态单独防护，且安全数据稀缺、微调成本高，难以应对复合攻击。

Method: 1）通过比较有害与良性样本的响应，识别单语言单模态的安全神经元，并用激活强度和下游影响量化其功能显著性；2）在同一语言内部取单模态安全神经元交集，得到视觉‑文本共享的模态安全神经元（MS‑Neurons）；3）以英语为语义锚，将各语言的MS‑Neurons取交集，得到跨语言跨模态的安全神经元（MLS‑Neurons）；4）仅对这少量共享神经元（约0.03% 参数）进行更新，实现从英文安全监督到多语言多模态的迁移。

Result: 在多语言多模态安全基准上，所提方法显著超越现有最先进技术，兼顾安全性提升与通用性能保持，实验验证了其在不同语言、不同视觉场景下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过在神经元层面发现并微调跨模态跨语言共享的安全神经元，能够高效、低成本地为大规模视觉语言模型提供复合攻击防御，展示了安全对齐的可扩展性和实用价值。

Abstract: As large vision-language models (LVLMs) are deployed globally, the combination of multilingual instructions and visual information makes malicious attacks more covert and sophisticated than ever before. However, existing methods isolate language and modality defenses, which, coupled with the scarcity of safety data and high fine-tuning costs, makes it difficult for models to defend against compound attacks. To address this severe challenge, we propose a neuron-level cross-dimensional safety alignment framework driven by modality- and language-shared safety neurons (MLS-Neurons). First, we identify monolingual and unimodal safety neurons by comparing responses to harmful and benign samples, quantifying functional saliency through activation strength and downstream impact. Then, by intersecting these unimodal neurons within each language, we extract modality-shared safety neurons (MS-Neurons) responsive to both visual and textual risks, bridging the safety representation gap between modalities. Furthermore, using English as a semantic anchor, we intersect MS-Neurons across languages to identify modality- and language-shared safety neurons (MLS-Neurons), serving as key defenses against compound attacks. Finally, we update only this minimal subset of shared neurons (~0.03% of parameters), transferring English-only safety supervision to multilingual and multimodal scenarios. Extensive experiments show that our method significantly outperforms state-of-the-art approaches across diverse multilingual and multimodal safety benchmarks while preserving general utility.

</details>


### [126] [Meta-Task: Turning Terminal Task Synthesis into a Terminal Task for Scalable Agent Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27929)
*Zhihong Pan,Jiyuan He,Kai Zhang,Yupeng Han,Ze Liu,Yuze Zhao,Yongcong Ye,Zhaohua Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Meta-Task 框架在真实容器环境中循环生成、执行、验证终端任务，仅用 3221 条合成轨迹即可显著提升 Qwen3 系列模型的 Pass@1 表现，优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 终端代理的规模化训练需要大量多样且可验证的任务和高质量交互轨迹，但现有合成方法因任务生成与真实执行脱节导致可靠性差，且依赖已有库限制了多样性和可扩展性，亟需更可靠、可扩展的任务合成方案。

Method: 提出 Meta-Task，将任务合成本身视为 Terminal‑Bench 格式任务：在真实容器中让代理循环生成、执行并验证任务，实现内部一致性检查。通过多维度解耦任务需求，采用多阶段机制先动态设计任务规格，再生成具体任务，并可引入外部材料提升多样性和真实感；最终使用 LLM‑as‑Judge 过滤确保数据质量。

Result: 在 Terminal‑Bench 2.0 上，仅使用 3,221 条 Meta-Task 合成轨迹，对 Qwen3-14B 微调后 Avg Pass@1 达到 22.5%，对 Qwen3-32B 达到 31.8%，显著超越同期方法且所需训练数据更少。

Conclusion: Meta-Task 有效解决了任务合成的可靠性和多样性问题，证明在真实执行环境中闭环生成任务能够大幅提升终端代理的训练效率和性能，为大规模终端代理训练提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Training terminal agents at scale requires diverse, verifiable terminal tasks and high-quality interaction trajectories, yet acquiring such data remains a significant challenge. Existing synthesis methods face two key limitations: (1) weak reliability caused by the disconnect between task generation and real execution, and (2) limited diversity and scalability due to dependence on existing repositories. We propose Meta-Task, a framework that redefines terminal task synthesis as a Terminal-Bench-format task itself: an agent operates within a real container environment to iteratively generate, execute, and verify tasks, so that synthesized components are checked for internal consistency and executability within the generation loop itself. Building upon this, we decouple the target task requirements along multiple dimensions, introduce a multi-phase mechanism that dynamically designs novel task specifications before producing the actual tasks, and incorporate optional external material support to enhance diversity and realism. We additionally apply LLM-as-Judge filtering to ensure the quality of the final training data. Experiments on Terminal-Bench 2.0 show that fine-tuning on only 3,221 Meta-Task synthesized trajectories achieves 22.5% and 31.8% Avg Pass@1 for Qwen3-14B and Qwen3-32B respectively, outperforming concurrent approaches with significantly less training data.

</details>


### [127] [The Geometric Nature and a Free Proxy for Flow-Matching Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27933)
*Ziyang Rao,Yiren Zhao,Weiyu Guo,Ben Fei,Yandong Guo,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于几何视角的 FM 不确定性度量——去噪加速度（accel），无需额外训练或采样，仅通过一次前向传播即可检测动作生成的潜在失误，且在实际部署中表现优于传统不确定性估计方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于 Flow Matching 的动作生成模型在面对场景误解或分布外输入时会产生错误的动作序列，但缺乏对其内部不确定性的显式表征，导致难以判断生成动作的可靠性；而现有的不确定性估计方法成本高、计算负担大且泛化能力不足。

Method: 作者从速度场的几何角度出发，将 FM 不确定性解释为偏离理想仿射各向同性收缩场的程度。基于此，提出去噪加速度（accel）作为不确定性代理：在单次前向传播中记录去噪轨迹的弯曲程度，无需额外模型评估、再采样或额外训练。

Result: 理论分析与大量实验表明，accel 能忠实反映 FM 的不确定性，并在在线故障检测任务中提前识别出即将失败的 rollout。相较于基于重采样或额外训练的基线方法，accel 在相同或更低的计算预算下实现了相当或更好的检测性能。

Conclusion: 去噪加速度提供了一种高效、通用且无额外成本的 FM 不确定性估计手段，能够在实际部署环境中实现及时的失败预警，为安全可靠的动作生成奠定了基础。

Abstract: Flow matching (FM) has become a popular action head paradigm for modern embodied models. However, as a conditional generative model, it does not explicitly expose its inherent uncertainty, producing faulty action chunks even when it misinterprets the scene or encounters out-of-distribution (OOD) inputs. Therefore, determining when an FM-generated action can be trusted is essential for safe deployment, yet existing uncertainty estimation methods on real-time control suffer from several issues: extra training budget, high computational overhead, and low generalization ability. In this work, we provide a geometric interpretation of FM uncertainty in the velocity field, showing that uncertainty manifests as deviation from an ideal affine-isotropic contraction field. Building on this observation, we introduce denoising acceleration ($\mathrm{accel}$), a highly-generalizable and cost-free uncertainty proxy that measures the bending of the denoising trajectory from a single forward pass, without additional model evaluations, training, or resampling. We theoretically and empirically demonstrate that $\mathrm{accel}$ is a faithful proxy for FM uncertainty and further test its utility in online failure detection. Results show that $\mathrm{accel}$ identifies failing rollouts well before termination, matching or even outperforming costly resampling- and training-based baselines across settings under realistic deployment budget. Code and demos available at: https://github.com/rrrrrrzy/fm-geometry.

</details>


### [128] [Shapes from Examples: Foundations of Shape Learning in Recursive SHACL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27934)
*Bente Gortworst,Cem Okulmus,Magdalena Ortiz,Anni-Yasmin Turhan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究在 SHACL 的 ELI 核心片段下，给定正负示例节点集合，如何在递归形状目录的不同语义（良基、稳定、支持）下求解形状表达式的拟合问题，并给出存在性与最特定拟合的时间复杂度上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在知识图谱中，SHACL 用于图数据的约束验证，自动学习符合给定正负示例的形状（shape）对于提升数据质量和应用效率至关重要。

Method: 作者将形状学习形式化为“拟合”问题：在 SHACL 的 ELI 片段中，利用递归形状目录的三种语义（well‑founded、stable、supported），分析正例集合 P 与负例集合 N 的约束满足情况，并设计算法求解是否存在满足条件的形状表达式以及最特定的解。

Result: 证明了拟合存在性和最特定拟合的计算均在指数时间内可解（tight upper bound），并在若干实际重要的特例（如非递归目录或限制形状结构）下实现多项式时间算法。

Conclusion: 在 SHACL‑ELI 片段中，形状学习的拟合问题虽在一般情况下具有指数复杂度，但在常见的受限场景下可高效求解，为知识图谱的自动约束生成提供了理论保障。

Abstract: SHACL shapes enable data graph validation, making automatic shape learning essential for knowledge graph applications. We investigate the well-known fitting approach to this task: given sets P and N of positive and negative example nodes from an input graph, compute a shape expression C, possibly using shape names defined in a recursive shape catalogue, that validates at every node in P and none in N. We focus on the case where C is written in a core fragment of SHACL corresponding to the Description Logic ELI. For the catalogue, we consider the well-founded, stable, and supported semantics. We address fitting existence and most specific fitting computation, establish tight exponential-time upper bounds for both problems, and obtain polynomial bounds for relevant special cases.

</details>


### [129] [From Scoring to Acting: Outcome-Verified Comparative Self-Distillation for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27937)
*Xu Xia,Jinghua Piao,Min Yang,Xiaochong Lan,Jiaju Chen,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Outcome-Verified Comparative Self-Distillation (OVCSD) 方法，通过在学生失败轨迹上构建前缀树、调用技能条件教师并仅保留经结果验证的成功续写，进行局部比较学习，实现对LLM代理的能力内化，显著提升在ALFWorld和WebShop上的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自蒸馏方法仅在学生生成的轨迹上对动作打分，缺乏环境结果的验证，且未充分利用学生与教师轨迹及其行为关系的信息，导致教师偏好可能不符合实际任务需求。

Method: OVCSD 首先将学生的失败回滚组织成前缀树，在学生到达的状态处自适应调用技能条件教师，只保留经结果验证的成功续写。随后在首次状态对齐的分歧点进行局部比较学习，将教师后缀的完成行为蒸馏给学生，实现基于结果验证的比较自蒸馏。

Result: 在 ALFWorld 和 WebShop 两个基准上，跨三种模型规模实验表明 OVCSD 相比最强基线分别提升了最高 29.7% 和 5.4% 的绝对成功率，并且训练期间仅增加不到 3% 的特权交互。

Conclusion: 通过结果验证的教师监督和教师‑学生轨迹的比较学习，OVCSD 有效提升了 LLM 代理的内部化能力，证明了在少量特权交互下即可显著超越传统强化学习和现有自蒸馏方法。

Abstract: Recent work on LLM agents is shifting from external capability elicitation to capability internalization, enabling agents to retain useful skills without retrieval at inference time. On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) offers a promising direction, but many existing methods typically supervise students by scoring actions along student-generated trajectories. Such supervision has two limitations: teacher preferences are not validated by environment outcomes, and action-level scores underuse information from student rollouts, teacher rollouts, and their behavioral relationship. We therefore advocate outcome-verified teacher supervision and comparative learning over teacher-student trajectories. Based on this view, we propose Outcome-Verified Comparative Self-Distillation (OVCSD). OVCSD organizes failed student rollouts into a prefix tree, adaptively invokes a skill-conditioned teacher from student-reached states, and retains only outcome-verified successful continuations. It then applies localized comparative learning at the first state-aligned divergence and distills the post-divergence teacher suffix to transfer completion behavior. Experiments on ALFWorld and WebShop across three model scales show that OVCSD consistently outperforms skill-free RL and existing self-distillation baselines, achieving up to 29.7 and 5.4 absolute success-rate gains over the strongest baselines on ALFWorld and WebShop, respectively, while adding less than 3% privileged interaction during training.

</details>


### [130] [MARS-RA: Rank Aggregation for Credit Assignment via Multimodal Comparisons in Embodied Multi-Agent Cooperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27967)
*Dawei Wang,Di Zhao,Xinyuan Liu,Marci Chi Ma,Xiaoyang Liu,Chengming Zhou,Gary Ushaw,Richard Davison*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 

Method: 

Result: 

Conclusion: 

Abstract: Credit assignment is a fundamental challenge in cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning, particularly in embodied AI settings characterized by limited and delayed feedback as well as dynamically changing numbers of active agents. We propose MARS-RA, a framework that reformulates credit assignment as a rank aggregation problem using contribution-based pairwise comparisons among agents generated by large multimodal models. This shift from absolute to relative estimation ensures robustness against noise and dynamic agent participation, converting comparison results into contribution scores for potential-based reward shaping. We provide theoretical justification for the convergence and robustness of the proposed framework, and show that Shapley values can be used as an interpretive reference. Experimental results on challenging tasks of different types indicate that MARS-RA can guide agents toward effective cooperation.

</details>


### [131] [Share the Judge, Learn the Deferral: Where Specialization Helps LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27984)
*Weining Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 

Method: 

Result: 

Conclusion: 

Abstract: Agentic systems have widened the gap between producing candidate outputs and reviewing them. This paper asks a practical architectural question: should domain specialization be built into an evaluator's weights, or into the rule that decides when its judgment can be trusted? We study 99,952 public, rubric-conditioned examples. Supplying the correct rubric improves locked-test accuracy by 2.11 points over a response-only control; replacing it with an unrelated rubric costs 2.66 points. Dividing the same training corpus among eight criterion-family LoRA judges, however, loses 10.05 points and cuts audited coverage at a 5% risk target from 24.44% to 5.43%. Matching the bank's stored capacity with one rank-64 adapter does not reproduce this loss. Nor is the result explained by learning rate or optimizer steps. Initializing the family adapters from a shared, trained judge recovers test accuracy to 76.85%, 19.94 points above scratch training at the same learning rate (95% interval 18.88-21.02). The result changes when specialization governs deferral rather than judgment. On RewardBench 2, learned correctness heads route examples through a 0.6B-4B-8B cascade without changing any reward score. Across 20 locked repartitions, the cascade attains 89.40% accuracy, compared with 84.75% for 8B alone, at 0.415 normalized parameter compute. Every run passes an exact one-sided 95% risk audit; margin-based rules remain near 84.8% accuracy while using at least 0.94 compute. These results suggest a qualified design rule: share the learning of judgment until there is enough data to justify a split, and place domain-specific adaptation in an audited release boundary.

</details>


### [132] [SKIMIX: Multi-Agent Harness-Time Scaling with Skill Mixture for Dynamic Harness Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27994)
*Jia Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SKIMIX 是一种多智能体框架，通过嵌入检索、子模稀释防止路由和自适应技能进化，实现技能库的协同迭代，显著提升开放式数学推理，但对选择题收益有限或负向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大规模技能库的出现，如何高效检索、组合并维护技能成为瓶颈；现有单一智能体难以充分利用多样化技能，亟需一种能够协同利用不同技能组合的框架。

Method: 提出 SKIMIX 框架：① 基于向量嵌入的技能检索；② 子模（submodular）反稀释路由策略，防止技能冗余稀释；③ 自适应技能进化机制，在每轮迭代中更新和扩展技能集合；多智能体在每轮迭代中相互协作并进行结果细化。

Result: 在六个推理基准上实验：对开放式数学推理任务，多智能体协作显著提升性能；对多项选择题的提升有限或出现负向效果。性能随智能体数量呈非单调变化，主要收益集中在首轮细化。

Conclusion: 任务属性决定技能级别集成的有效性；对需要深度推理的开放式任务，多智能体技能协同有明显优势；而对结构化选择题收益有限。研究为可扩展的智能体设计提供了实践指引。

Abstract: AI agents increasingly rely on large skill libraries, but selecting, combining, and maintaining skills remains difficult. We propose SKIMIX, a multi-agent framework in which agents with different skill portfolios collaborate through iterative refinement. SKIMIX combines embedding-based skill retrieval, submodular anti-dilution routing, and adaptive skill evolution. Across six reasoning benchmarks, multi-agent collaboration substantially improves open-ended mathematical reasoning but offers limited or negative gains on multiple-choice tasks. Agent-count scaling is non-monotonic, and most improvements arise during the first refinement round. These results show that task characteristics determine whether skill-level ensembles help and provide practical guidance for scalable agent design.

</details>


### [133] [MMLDSum-LLM: Multimodal Long-Document Summarization with Visual-Alignment and Keyword-Aware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28006)
*Xianpeng Zhang,Jiahua Yang,Dongyu Chen,Lei zhang,Jian Ma,Xu guohuan,Haonan Lu,Tianhuang Su,Chuangchuang Wang,Kai Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MMLDSum-Bench基准和MMLDSum-LLM两阶段训练框架，显著提升多模态长文档摘要的关键信息覆盖和跨模态一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态长文档摘要中关键信息稀疏分布，导致摘要遗漏和跨模态幻觉，根源在于长程依赖注意力漂移和模态对齐不足。

Method: 构建覆盖多领域、多长度和视觉‑文本分布的MMLDSum-Bench基准；提出MMLDSum-LLM框架：①监督微调阶段加入视觉对齐加权损失和关键词感知加权损失；②基于GRPO的强化学习阶段使用多目标奖励（关键词覆盖、图文对齐、ROUGE、长度控制）。

Result: 在统一评估协议下（LLM‑as‑judge、原子主张精召、图文对齐ITA、ROUGE），MMLDSum-LLM相较于主流闭源/开源多模态模型显著提升关键内容覆盖率、跨模态一致性和摘要质量。

Conclusion: 通过高质量基准和两阶段训练策略，可有效缓解注意力漂移和模态对齐缺口，推动多模态长文档摘要性能的实质性提升。

Abstract: Multimodal long documents are core carriers of professional knowledge, where critical evidence is sparsely distributed across paragraphs and modalities. This easily causes key information omission and cross-modal hallucinations in summarization by multimodal LLMs. These issues stem from attention drift in long-range dependency modeling and gaps in inter-modal alignment. To address this, we introduce MMLDSum-Bench, a high-quality benchmark for multimodal long-document summarization, covering multiple domains, context-length scales, and visual-textual modality distributions. We further propose MMLDSum-LLM, a reproducible two-stage training framework that combines supervised fine-tuning with visual-alignment weighted loss and keyword-aware weighted loss, followed by GRPO with a multi-objective reward (keyword coverage, image-text alignment, ROUGE, and length control). Extensive experiments on MMLDSum-Bench, comparing against leading closed-source and open-source multimodal models under a unified evaluation protocol - including LLM-as-a-judge scoring, atomic-claim precision/recall, image-text alignment (ITA), and ROUGE - demonstrate that our approach significantly improves key-information coverage and cross-modal consistency.

</details>


### [134] [MUL-T: Decoding Spatial Cellular Architecture in Multiplexed Tissue Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28030)
*Farzaneh Seyedshahi,Kai Rakovic,Adalberto Claudio Quiros,John LeQuesne,Ke Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MUL-T是一种轻量级Transformer框架，将组织结构建模为离散细胞标记的掩码上下文预测任务，能够在多种临床下游任务中以更少参数和计算成本实现与大型ViT相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多重成像组织分析方法依赖手工特征，难以在标记面板异质的不同队列间扩展和泛化，需要一种能够自动捕获细胞间高阶交互且计算高效的模型。

Method: MUL-T将组织切片转化为离散的细胞token序列，采用轻量级Transformer进行掩码上下文预测，学习无监督的[CLS]嵌入以捕获细胞空间关系，整个框架无需任务特定监督。

Result: 在核心级肿瘤模式分类、患者级分级、PD‑L1阳性预测以及跨数据集治疗响应预测等任务上，MUL‑T consistently 超越传统特征基线，并在参数量和训练成本远低于基础ViT模型的情况下，取得相近的性能。

Conclusion: MUL‑T提供了一种高效且可迁移的组织结构表征方法，能够在多任务临床应用中实现优于手工特征的表现，展示了轻量Transformer在多重成像分析中的潜力。

Abstract: Understanding tissue organisation in multiplexed imaging requires modelling both cellular phenotypes and their spatial context. Existing approaches typically rely on handcrafted features, such as marker intensity statistics or cell-type proportions, which often fail to scale or generalise across cohorts with heterogeneous marker panels. We introduce MUL-T, a lightweight transformer framework that reframes tissue architecture as a masked contextual prediction task over discrete cell tokens. By learning contextualised [CLS] embeddings without task-specific supervision, the model captures higher-order cellular interactions while remaining computationally efficient. We evaluate MUL-T on several clinically relevant downstream tasks, including core-level tumour pattern classification, patient-level grading, PD-L1 positivity prediction, and cross-dataset treatment response prediction. Across tasks, MUL-T consistently outperforms classical feature-based baselines and achieves performance comparable to a foundation ViT model, despite substantially fewer parameters and lower training cost.

</details>


### [135] [DataClawEval: A Benchmark for Data Engineering Agents in Real Industrial Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28033)
*Debin Meng,Jiaming Yang,Zefang Zong,Tengyue Xu,Haining Xie,Yang Li,Peng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DataClawEval 是首个面向真实数据工程全流程的基准，包含 100 条跨五种执行引擎的任务，使用确定性沙箱评估 16 种 LLM 代理，最高得分仅 74.9，显示当前模型在数据工程上仍有显著局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评测主要聚焦于简化的 Text-to-SQL 或数据分析，未覆盖真实企业级数据工程的端到端任务，导致对 LLM 代理在实际数据工程场景中的能力缺乏了解。

Method: 构建 DataClawEval 基准：收集专业数据工程师编写的生产级代码，设计 100 条覆盖 PySpark、MySQL、HiveSQL、PrestoSQL/Trino、FlinkSQL 的完整工作流。每条任务在独立容器沙箱中执行，使用规则化脚本进行确定性评分。对 16 种前沿 LLM 代理进行评测。

Result: 评测显示最强模型整体得分仅 74.9%，且不同模型在不同执行引擎上表现各异，未出现全能模型，表明当前 LLM 代理在数据工程领域存在显著的专业化局限。

Conclusion: 自动化数据工程仍是一个未解决的难题，现有 LLM 代理缺乏跨引擎的通用能力。DataClawEval 为后续研究提供了真实、可复现的评测平台。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) and LLM-based agents are increasingly being deployed to automate complex workflows, promising to revolutionize data management and processing. However, existing benchmarks predominantly focus on simplified Text-to-SQL translation or data analysis, leaving the critical and complex domain of end-to-end data engineering largely unexplored. To bridge this gap, we introduce DataClawEval, the first comprehensive benchmark designed specifically to evaluate the end-to-end task completion capabilities of autonomous agents in real-world data engineering scenarios. Built upon production-grade code authored by professional enterprise data engineers, it comprises 100 rigorous, end-to-end tasks spanning five execution engines: PySpark, MySQL, HiveSQL, PrestoSQL/Trino, and FlinkSQL. Rather than non-deterministic LLM-as-a-judge scoring, each task is executed within a case-specific, isolated sandbox and graded by deterministic, rule-based scripts. Evaluating 16 frontier agents exposes critical limitations: The strongest model attains only 74.9 overall, and no single model dominates, as each excels on a different engine, revealing strict domain specialization rather than omnipotent proficiency. Thus, autonomous data engineering remains a formidable, unresolved challenge. We release our dataset, containerized environments, and deterministic evaluation scripts at https://github.com/Dicemy/DataClawEval/tree/master

</details>


### [136] [SKILL-KD: Contrastive Skill Distillation for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28048)
*Qiming Shi,Yibo Dou,Jiawen Zhu,Yulong Tao,Linbo Jin,Zhaolu Kang,Yunfan Zhou,Di Weng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SKILL-KD对比式技能蒸馏框架，通过学生失败轨迹与教师轨迹的差异生成文本技能补丁并迭代修正，防止技能漂移，实现学生代理的显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能获取方法将技能视为经验或示例摘要，导致学生代理在缺乏任务知识或操作策略时难以从失败轨迹中提取有效信息，教师轨迹又过于隐式，难以转化为可复用的指导。

Method: SKILL-KD将技能视为不同能力代理之间的显式蒸馏介质：① 给定学生失败和教师成功的同任务轨迹，提取可操作的差异并生成文本技能补丁；② 通过重新运行学生验证补丁效果，若仍失败则迭代细化；③ 为防止局部更新导致技能漂移，维护轨迹关联的编辑历史，并采用漂移感知技能合并（Drift-Aware Skill Consolidation），决定是新增、删除、修改规则或跳过。

Result: 在五个代理基准和两种学生设置下，SKILL-KD对冻结的学生模型持续提升性能，优于固定模型适应的基线方法。

Conclusion: 将技能显式化为可编辑的文本规则并通过对比蒸馏与漂移感知合并，可有效弥补学生代理的知识缺口，实现跨能力层级的稳健技能迁移。

Abstract: Skill-based prompting has become a practical mechanism for improving large language model (LLM) agents, yet existing skill acquisition methods often treat skills as experience summaries, memory entries, or direct summaries of successful demonstrations. This creates a mismatch for weaker student agents: when a student fails because it lacks task knowledge or operational strategy, its failed trajectory may not contain enough evidence to infer the missing behavior, while the teacher trajectory may be too implicit to be internalized as reusable guidance. We propose SKILL-KD, a contrastive skill distillation framework that treats skills as an explicit distillation medium between agents of different capabilities. Given a student failure and the teacher trajectory on the same task, SKILL-KD distills their actionable discrepancy into a textual skill patch, evaluates the patch by re-running the student, and iteratively refines the patch when the student still fails. To prevent repeated local updates from causing skill drift, SKILL-KD further maintains trace-linked edit histories and performs Drift-Aware Skill Consolidation, deciding whether each patch should add a new rule, delete or modify an existing rule, or be skipped. Across five agent benchmarks and two student settings, SKILL-KD consistently improves frozen student agents over fixed-model adaptation baselines.

</details>


### [137] [IndustryForge-27B: A Domain-Enhanced Multimodal Foundation Model for Industrial CAD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28050)
*Nianchen Deng,Jiaxin Ai,Tao Hu,Shu Zou,Yurui Dong,Siqi Li,Xinyu Cai,Xuemeng Yang,Licheng Wen,Hongbin Zhou,Hairong Zhang,Pinlong Cai,Botian Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 构建了IndustryForge-27B模型，基于Qwen3.5-VL-27B，整合约52k工业CAD多模态样本，统一多任务微调，使模型在CAD相关基准上提升33.65个百分点，且在通用能力基准上保持或略有提升，超越闭源GPT-5.4。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业CAD设计与制造需要模型同时理解工程图纸和3D几何截图，生成参数化建模脚本和Windows COM API代码，并覆盖从单件到装配的全流程。现有通用多模态模型在这些任务上表现不足，单任务微调又过于狭窄，难以支撑上层智能体的多样调用。

Method: 在Qwen3.5-VL-27B基础上，收集并整合六个工业CAD子语料库（CAD-VQA、text2cadquery、text2cadquery-assembly、com_2d、com_3d、com_assembly），共约52k多模态样本。采用统一的多任务监督微调（SFT）策略，对模型进行训练，使其同时掌握视觉问答、参数化代码生成、装配代码生成以及Inventor/SolidWorks的COM接口代码编写。

Result: 在四个CAD领域基准测试中，IndustryForge-27B相较基模型平均提升33.65个百分点，并在所有基准上超越闭源模型GPT-5.4。在十一项通用能力基准上，模型保持原有性能并略有提升（平均+1.56个百分点），未出现灾难性遗忘。

Conclusion: IndustryForge-27B提供了面向工业CAD全流程的统一基础模型，可作为下游工业智能体的通用底座，支持从零件设计到装配、从单次生成到闭环自我改进的完整工业软件操作。

Abstract: Automating industrial CAD design and manufacturing places distinctive demands on multimodal foundation models: the model must see engineering drawings and 3D geometry screenshots, write correct parametric-modelling scripts and Windows COM API code, and cover the full range from single parts to assemblies. General-purpose multimodal models fall short on these tasks, while single-task fine-tuning is too narrow to support the diverse calls that upper-layer agents issue. We build IndustryForge-27B on top of Qwen3.5-VL-27B by curating and integrating six industrial-CAD sub-corpora totalling $\sim$52k multimodal samples---CAD Visual QA (CAD-VQA), parametric CAD code (text2cadquery), assembly-level CAD code (text2cadquery-assembly), and three COM sub-corpora for Inventor / SolidWorks (com_2d / com_3d / com_assembly)---and training with a unified multi-task SFT recipe. Across four CAD-domain benchmarks IndustryForge-27B lifts the base model by $+33.65$~pp on average and outperforms the strong closed-source model GPT-5.4 on all four; across eleven general-capability benchmarks it retains, and slightly improves upon, the base model ($+1.56$~pp mean, no catastrophic forgetting). IndustryForge-27B will serve as the common substrate for downstream industrial-agent projects, providing a unified starting point for a full-stack industrial agent that spans from CAD design to industrial-software operation, from parts to assemblies, and from single-shot generation to closed-loop self-improvement.

</details>


### [138] [SemPIC: Learning Semantic Position-Independent KV Caches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28069)
*Hui Xie,Peng Xiao,Yutong Deng\textsuperscript,Shuoran Dou,Jian Yang,Jinyang Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SemPIC方法，通过在离线阶段使用LoRA Writer对文档KV进行学习式编译，实现位置无关缓存的可靠复用，显著提升长上下文检索任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在长上下文检索和需要多轮指令变化的任务中，同一文档会被多次复用，但传统前缀缓存无法利用这种复用，位置无关缓存因缺乏未来上下文而不可靠，需要一种能够在保持KV接口不变的前提下，提高缓存可复用性的方案。

Method: SemPIC 将预训练的解码器保持为不变的Reader，离线训练一个 LoRA‑enabled Writer，通过行为蒸馏让其在每层生成文档的 KV 表示；同时引入 KV 梯度检查点技术，在训练时对缓存的 KV 进行梯度检查点以降低显存占用而不切断梯度流。

Result: 在3个模型和4个任务上的实验表明，SemPIC 将 KV Packet 的平均 micro‑F1 从 0.53 提升至 0.60，接近全重新计算的 0.62。

Conclusion: SemPIC 通过对文档表示的适配，实现了可靠的位置信息无关缓存，兼顾了离线缓存构建的灵活性和解码时的高效性，为长上下文检索和多指令工作负载提供了实用的性能提升方案。

Abstract: Long-context retrieval and agentic workloads repeatedly reuse the same documents under changing instructions, histories, and document orders. Prefix caching cannot exploit this reuse, while position-independent caching (PIC) remains unreliable because independently compiled KV states lack the future context in which they will be consumed. Our diagnostics show that a learned boundary-conditioned baseline sharply reduces attention deviation near reusable-block boundaries but leaves interior and task-level residuals, motivating adaptation of the document representation itself. We present \emph{SemPIC}, which trains a LoRA-enabled Writer to compile native per-layer document KVs through behavioral distillation while retaining the pretrained decoder as an unchanged Reader. Adaptation is confined to offline cache construction, preserving the standard KV interface and cache-hit decoding path. We further introduce KV Gradient Checkpointing, which reduces peak training memory without severing gradients through cached KVs. Across three models and four tasks, SemPIC raises mean micro-F1 over KV Packet from 0.53 to 0.60, approaching Full Recompute at 0.62.

</details>


### [139] [Echoverse: Deep, Evolving Environments for Training Computer-Use Agents at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28074)
*Yash Pandya,Sahil Gupta,Sarthak Harne,Archana Yadav,Kavyansh Chourasia,Hussein Mozannar,Vibhav Vineet,Sara Abdali,Corby Rosset,Yash Lara,Ahmed Awadallah,Ece Kamar,Akshay Nambi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Echoverse 将规范编译为有状态的合成应用，并通过共进化循环利用环境修复和模型训练，实现在12个环境上9B模型的显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的计算机使用代理需要在真实的登录受限且有状态的应用上进行训练，但获取大量此类环境成本高；因此需要高效生成并利用合成环境来提升模型学习效果。

Method: 提出 Echoverse 框架：① 将任务规范编译成带数据库评分的有状态应用；② 通过共进化循环，对每次模型 rollout 进行双重读取，既用于修复环境、任务和验证器，又作为模型的训练信号；③ 在训练中评估环境的行为深度、错误交互目标以及与模型的协同提升。

Result: 在12个生成的环境上，9B模型的整体准确率从 36.5% 提升至 67.1%，接近更大模型的水平；深度环境提升现场准确率至 85%（对比基线 80%），浅层环境则下降；单环境修复将模型准确率从 16.2% 提升至 38.5%；在强化学习设置下，结合验证器的奖励将持出分数从 58.8% 提升至 68.0%。

Conclusion: 环境的行为深度、针对模型失误的设计以及与模型的协同进化是提升计算机使用代理性能的关键。Echoverse 提供了可复用的合成环境基准，展示了通过共进化修复与训练相结合的有效路径。

Abstract: Computer-use agents learn from what their actions change, so training one needs applications it can act on, break and reset. The applications that matter most are login-gated and stateful, so synthetic environments stand in for them. Recent pipelines generate such environments in bulk, which moves the bottleneck from how many exist to what is inside each one. The returns, we find, come from three properties: how much behavioural depth an environment carries, whether it targets the interaction an agent actually fails, and whether it improves alongside the model. We present Echoverse, which compiles specifications into stateful applications whose tasks are graded against the application's own database, and a co-evolution loop that reads every graded rollout twice: as repairs to the environment, its tasks and its verifier, and as training signal for the model. Trained on twelve such environments, a 9B model improves from $36.5\%$ to $67.1\%$ across fourteen evaluation splits, within fourteen points of the much larger frontier model that taught it. We examine each property in turn. On the same domains, shallow environments push live-site accuracy below the base model ($80.0 \to 75.0$) while deep ones raise it ($80.0 \to 85.0$ and $48.0 \to 65.0$); drilling one interface control across many renderings transfers to held-out widget families and to the open web; and repairing a single environment lifts the model trained on it from $16.2\%$ to $38.5\%$. The same worlds serve as reinforcement-learning environments, where a reward combining the grounded verifier with a dense per-step judge raises held-out score from $58.8\%$ to $68.0\%$. We release four environments as a benchmark, with their applications, seed data and grounded graders. Code: https://aka.ms/echoverse

</details>


### [140] [SciDataSailor: Deep Scientific Data Exploring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28098)
*Jiyong Rao,Yicheng Qiu,Chi Zhang,Chunfeng Song,Runkai Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Deep Scientific Data Exploration任务范式，并实现SciDataSailor框架，通过MCTS生成工具交互轨迹，实现对层次化科学数据仓库的自动探索、分析与结论生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM研究虽在规划、推理和工具使用上取得进展，但几乎未涉及在可执行环境中直接与异构、相互依赖的科学数据集交互，导致数据检查、整合和分析仍需大量人工和领域专业知识。

Method: 构建SciDataSailor框架，将轨迹合成建模为蒙特卡罗树搜索（MCTS），并引入四个任务专属机制：难度分层的探索种子、双向反馈的首轮紧迫感、层次化的策略到工具动作映射、基于熵的分支控制。基于此生成SciDataSailor‑SFT‑2K用于监督微调，构建SciDataSailor‑Bench评估集。

Result: SciDataSailor‑Bench包含27个生命、地球和物理科学数据集，共1133个任务（627个元信息摘要任务 + 586个科学问答任务），用于评估模型在跨文件证据整合和基于执行观察的推理能力。

Conclusion: 通过MCTS驱动的探索‑利用平衡，SciDataSailor展示了LLM代理在真实科学数据仓库中进行自动化探索、分析和结论生成的可行性，为后续大模型在科学数据交互领域的研究提供了任务范式和基准。

Abstract: Scientific datasets are commonly organized as hierarchical repositories containing heterogeneous and interdependent files, making their inspection, integration, and analysis labor-intensive and reliant on domain expertise. Although large language model (LLM) agents have advanced substantially in planning, reasoning, and tool use, existing research has largely overlooked their ability to interact with real scientific data assets through executable environments. We introduce Deep Scientific Data Exploration, an agentic task paradigm in which agents navigate repositories, interpret heterogeneous files and schemas, execute analyses, integrate cross-file evidence, and produce conclusions grounded in executed observations. To operationalize this paradigm, we present SciDataSailor, a framework for synthesizing tool-interactive trajectories by balancing broad exploration with targeted exploitation. SciDataSailor instantiates trajectory synthesis as Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) with four task-specific mechanisms: difficulty-stratified exploration seeds, dual-feedback first-play urgency, hierarchical strategy-to-tool action generation, and entropy-guided branching. Using this framework, we construct SciDataSailor-SFT-2K for supervised fine-tuning and SciDataSailor-Bench for evaluation, with the latter comprising 627 meta-information summarization tasks and 586 scientific question-answering tasks across 27 datasets spanning the life, earth, and physical sciences.

</details>


### [141] [Group-Reflective Self-Distillation for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28076)
*Binbin Zheng,Zijun Xie,Guanqun Zhao,Enlei Gong,Xing Ma,Xiaoliang Fu,Zeyu Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了基于策略自身已验证轨迹的群体反思自蒸馏（GRSD）方法，通过对成功与失败轨迹的对比生成特权指导，提升了大语言模型在强化学习任务中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 终端奖励仅提供粗粒度的轨迹监督，导致成功行为、重复错误和偶然选择混杂；现有自蒸馏方法依赖外部或单轨迹技能，可能与当前经验不匹配或超出策略能力，限制了学习效果。

Method: GRSD 在同一策略的已验证轨迹组内进行反思：对每个提示，策略对每条已验证轨迹生成反思；使用停止梯度的快照将成功与失败轨迹的反思进行对比，构建群体级特权指导；在此指导下，自教师通过调节基于结果的优势函数进行逐回合信用分配，同时保持验证器确定的学习方向。

Result: 在多个代理环境和不同模型规模的实验中，GRSD 相比竞争基线持续取得更高的成功率，并在未见任务上表现出更强的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 利用策略自身的已验证轨迹进行群体反思可提供能力对齐且结果区分的指导，显著提升强化学习中大语言模型的学习效率和泛化性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) is effective for training large language model agents. However, terminal rewards provide only coarse trajectory-level supervision, leaving successful behaviors, recurring mistakes, and incidental choices entangled in the same outcome signal. Existing agentic self-distillation methods enrich sparse supervision with natural-language skills, but skills retrieved externally or extracted from a single trajectory by stronger models may mismatch current experience, exceed the policy's capability, or remain path-specific.
